### Node.js AI-Generated Images: 3 Rules to Compress and Upload to Cloud Storage Explained

![Node.js AI-Generated Images: 3 Rules to Compress and Upload to Cloud Storage Explained](<https://media2.dev.to/dynamic/image/width=1200,height=627,fit=cover,gravity=auto,format=auto/https%3A%2F%2Fdev-to-uploads.s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com%2Fuploads%2Farticles%2F1a5gtiws6eec0gt52y7t.png>)
